# Full category championship report

## A. Executive category decisions

**New periods:** authentic provider-labelled Week 6 and Week 7 rolling snapshots now cover **14 games each**,670 and 649thresholds. Original recorded selections total **445/801 (55.56%)** on this explicitly partial two-week panel: Over236/446(52.91%), Under209/355(58.87%). Week 6 alone is199/393(50.64%), versus Week 7 rolling246/408(60.29%). The preserved Friday-only Week 7 result183/279(65.59%) is a different source/horizon, excluded from these cumulative totals.

**New methods:** retained actual empirical, HGB objectives/specializations, base Leaf-CDF, C02, D1/D2, GLM laws and three chronological selector policies are evaluated where their exact inputs qualify. This is not a comparison of only five saved-point rules. Complete-law probability products are separate from point-versus-line choices. No new fits or model predict/apply calls; learned Leaf/C02 laws are recovered by lookup from retained histograms/leafIDs and checked against saved moments.

**Coverage limit:** numerical forecasts retain the fixed 2026 W1–7/107-game panel and supported full 2022–2025 seasons. Authentic qualified offered-threshold comparisons currently cover the newly stated2026W6–7 snapshots; W1–5 and historical seasons do not acquire invented quotes. No full 2026 season-to-date original-pick or multiseason-offer claim is made. Known strict HGB/D1D2 fit cells and precise source gaps remain visible. All seasons are exposed research.

The side maxima below are observed maxima over each procedure’s own eligible selected pool, with sample/coverage shown. Their selected cases differ. They are not a pure learner comparison or a deployment policy; tiny perfect subsets are flagged by their actual denominators. The executive side/probability maxima require a procedure to have both Week 6 and Week 7 source views; one-week partial maxima remain in the full weekly tables. Complete ties and all losing methods remain in the machine tables. Only the registered rolling product is pooled; fixed-Friday rows stay separate. No new minimum-support gate is invented.

Receptions has the preserved D2/C02 static tie. Chart-PPR ranks only the supported qualified labels; unresolved rare-component completeness prevents an unrestricted exact-chart or website-fantasy parity claim. Native units differ and must not be ranked against each other. Every winner above is best among the verified comparable tested procedures on its stated product/panel.

A challenger may advance provisionally through the retained owner under the existing authorized policy without a new blanket wait or an invented universal website-relative-win prerequisite. This does not establish a verified website improvement or scientific qualification. Own source/model recovery, identity/target/functional, inference, consumer, fallback, freshness, rollback and owner admission remain mandatory. ## Matched actual-line probability comparisons

Unlike the raw own-pool maxima, these contrasts use the same history, feature-memory policy and exact offers. Across Weeks 6–7, H-MULTI base Leaf-CDF passing-attempt half3-class Brier is **0.264387 versus GLM 0.360829** on **52 identical offers**, delta **−0.096442**, game95% sensitivity **[−0.151591, −0.041292]**. C02 is 0.272847 on the same 52 offers. Interceptions reverse: Leaf 0.248148 versus GLM 0.247129, delta +0.001019, interval crossing zero. Only two week clusters are available, so week-level uncertainty remains insufficient; these are exposed, multiplicity-unadjusted comparisons. Tiny D1/D2 fallback-law maxima above do not establish learned-law superiority. [All 480 paired contrasts and source masks](roster/paired-probability-v1/PAIRED_PROBABILITY_COMPARISONS.csv).
